## Working rules

- Build and verify in the separate API Preview. Preserve normal optimized208 and wrapper.
- Test messages only in the uniquely resolved Peter DM; use fixed synthetic data.
- Other conversations stay read-only. Existing bounded channel96 test is already spent.
- Use development official-session import in memory; no new saved credentials, revocation,
  raw session logs, or automatic Keychain prompts during preview rebuilds.
- Retain source/fixture/live/UI evidence separately. Never count unit-test passes as
  full parity or turn these checkbox totals into a completion percentage.
